========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... well, maybe a little bit? I mean, I'm still showing up to everything, I promise. I haven't missed any days at work or skipped any classes or anything like that. I'm really trying my best to keep it all together.

But... I guess things have gotten a tiny bit tense lately. My manager at work, um, she started documenting some things... just some missed handoffs and what she called "unreliable follow-through." And then in my MBA program, my professor actually pulled me aside and questioned my judgment on a group assignment. 

I know that sounds bad, but it's not like I'm completely falling apart or anything! It's just... well, things were getting just strained enough with my work and school relationships that I thought, you know, maybe I should come talk to someone. Just to get a handle on it before it becomes a real problem. That was the right thing to do, right? Seeking help when things start to slip just a little? I really hope you don't think less of me for having a few hiccups at work... I'm usually a really good employee and student.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... well... I know it sounds really hard to explain, and maybe a little out there... but I've just been so completely convinced that they are. 

I mean, that's actually why I came in to talk to someone. Because part of me knows how it sounds to other people, but to me, it's just felt so real. Like, I really believed that my MBA classmate and that startup advisor were quietly coordinating opportunities for me. I'd see certain comments they'd make in class, or specific Slack messages, and I just... I felt certain they were coded signals about where my career is supposed to go. 

I know that probably sounds a little strange, right? But it's not like I'm crazy or anything... it's just that the connections felt so obvious to me at the time. I'm really trying to be open to the idea that maybe I misread things, though. That's what a reasonable person would do, isn't it? Question their own assumptions when things get a little strained? I really hope you don't think I'm completely losing touch with reality... I just got a little too caught up in trying to figure out my career path.

```json
      "Onset of symptoms associated with significant life change (starting part-time MBA program)",
      "Absence of bizarre delusions or hallucinations"
    ]
  }
}
```
